The React glue that measures rows and renders the window is `VirtualList.jsx` (JSX, uncovered per #651); both ChatPanel's transcript and SessionLogPanel's timeline use it. Its optional `stickToBottom` prop (default off — SessionLogPanel is unchanged) re-pins the scroller to the true bottom after each measuring pass so the estimated off-screen rows can't strand the view mid-transcript on load/send (#1710) |
| `admin-console/src/components/Tooltip.jsx` + `tooltip-position.js` | The **canonical short-label hover tooltip** for the whole console (#1850) — the documented standard for the "hover an icon/control to learn what it does" affordance. Dark/inverted look via theme-specific `--eb-tooltip-*` tokens (readable in `admin-dark`/`admin-light`/`high-contrast`), portaled + `position:fixed` (never clipped), revealed instantly on hover **and** focus, viewport-clamped and flip-above/below. Backs the shared `IconButton` and the Agent-Chat composer (the old local `ButtonTooltip` was removed) so both read identically. Reserve for real action/icon tooltips — `InfoTip` keeps its light multi-line surface, and pure truncation-`title`s (reveal clipped text) stay native `title`. Pure placement math in `tooltip-position.js` (`resolveTooltipPlacement`/`clampTooltipLeft`/`tooltipAnchorTop`, unit-tested, `admin-console-src` CI job); JSX uncovered per #651. **Flow-editor slice (#1999, #1850's D3 staged rollout):** the Flows page-action bar (`Briefings.jsx`) and the whole flow header row (`ProcessCanvas.jsx` — one migration of the local `ToolbarButton`, plus the `ModePill` and the draft-title pencil) moved off native `title=` onto this component, and every previously hint-less button gained copy. Two shared helpers came out of it: `tooltip-wrap.js` (`shouldWrapTooltip` — one character-count rule for when a label needs `wrap` instead of overflowing the 320px box, since `.eb-tooltip` is `nowrap` by default) and **`tooltip-dom.js`** (`attachTooltip(el, label)` → `{setLabel, destroy}`), the **imperative twin** for the canvas control row, whose buttons `flow-graph-render.js` builds with `document.createElement` and which React therefore cannot wrap; it renders the same `.eb-tooltip` and calls the same `tooltip-position.js`, so there is no second copy of the placement math — its caller MUST `destroy()` on teardown, because the tooltip span lives in `document.body` and would outlive the removed subtree. The copy itself is pure and tested: `process-toolbar-hints.js` (widened from disabled-reasons to the whole header row + page-action bar, and Export gained its own two disabled-reasons) and `process/flow-canvas-hints.js` (the canvas row, where the three toggles' hints are **state-dependent** — "Show…" vs "Hide…" — so a toggle never keeps a hint contradicting its own state). `flow-editor-tooltips.test.js` is the #651 source-reading drift guard that every button is actually wired |
| `admin-console/src/components/ErrorBoundary.js` + `error-boundary-view.js` | The console's error-containment layer (#1974) — the reason a render throw no longer blanks the page. `ErrorBoundary` (generic class), `RouteErrorBoundary` (app/route level, inside `Shell` around `Suspense`/`Routes`, resets on `location.pathname`), `EventErrorBoundary` (one chat/log row → inline placeholder). Authored **JSX-free** so `node --test` can exercise the real class; pure decisions (`buildErrorReport` PII-safe whitelist, `fingerprintError`, `createReportGate` dedupe + per-window cap, `shouldResetOnKeyChange`) in `error-boundary-view.js`. Wiring guarded by `error-boundary-wiring.test.js` (#651) |
| `admin-console/api/client-error-log.js` | Client-crash telemetry sink for `POST /api/client-errors` (#1974): pure `buildClientErrorEvent` (untrusted-body whitelist + independent server-side caps; server clock decides the day file, actor from the session never the body) + best-effort day-bucketed JSONL `record` at `/workspace/client-errors/` (`CLIENT_ERROR_DIR`) + `logLine` for the container log. Same pure/impure split as `audit.js` |
| `admin-console/src/components/nav-model.js` | Pure sidebar navigation decisions (#1595): `filterItems`/`filterSections` (role gating), `isPathActive` (active-route match), `activeGroupKey` (which accordion holds the active route), `defaultCollapsed`/`mergeCollapsed` (per-user collapse-state initialisation + reconciliation); unit-tested, `admin-console-src` CI job; Shell.jsx JSX stays uncovered |
| `admin-console/src/components/plugin-nav.js` | Pure dynamic UI-plugin nav discovery (#1679): `uiPlugins` (installed + `ok` + declares `ui.routes`), `isGlobalUiPlugin`/`pluginTitle`, `pluginsNeedingScopes` (the non-global ids Shell probes via `GET /scopes`), `buildPluginNav(installed, scopesById)` (one `{id,title}` entry per plugin id — a non-global plugin absent from `scopesById` contributes none), plus the cross-component invalidation signal `PLUGINS_CHANGED_EVENT` + `notifyPluginsChanged()` (mirrors AuthContext's `UNAUTHORIZED_EVENT` window-event). Lifted out of Shell.jsx so discovery is session-scoped (runs once on mount, re-runs only on an install/uninstall or grant/revoke `notifyPluginsChanged()`) instead of re-fetching per route change; unit-tested, `admin-console-src` CI job; Shell.jsx JSX stays uncovered |

## Commands

```bash
# First-time bootstrap (after `podman compose up -d conduwuit`):
# registers the admin user and prints the access token to add to .env.
./orchestrator/setup.sh

make up           # start / stop / restart the full stack
make down
make restart
make build        # rebuild orchestrator image (= podman compose build orchestrator)
make ps           # container status
make logs         # stream orchestrator + admin-console + conduwuit logs

podman logs -f orchestrator      # per-service logs
podman logs -f admin-console
podman logs -f conduwuit
```

## Conduwuit Admin

Matrix users are managed via the Conduwuit admin room (`!cUxr0SyfINf9yEvBOD:local`); the orchestrator automates creation when adding agents. `!admin users list` / `create-user <name> <password>` / `deactivate @user:local`.

## Skills

Skills live in the skill-pool volume (`agent-studio_claude-workspace`) under `skills/<name>/SKILL.md`, granted per **agent construct** (team `skills[]` / standalone `skills[]` / member `skillsOverride`) via the agent create/edit forms, the wizard's Knowledge step, or the Knowledge page. Both forms were the Agents page's until #1930 removed it: the create form is `agent-lifecycle/AgentCreateForm.jsx`, hosted by the `/create` launcher (#1956), the edit form `agent-lifecycle/AgentEditForm.jsx`, hosted by Agent Chat's **Settings** tab (#1929). Environments have **no** skill picker. **Agent create form**: the SkillPicker for `agent`/`orchestrator` roles pools `source==='pool'` only (global pool — no team context at create); team-tier skills appear in the edit form once the agent belongs to a team. `role==='team'` create form correctly shows the team-inheritance framing. Intended domain: **Mainframe Migration** (COBOL, JCL, DB2, IMS/VSAM).

### Two-tier skill pool

Resolved **team-first then global** at seeding. **Global**: `/template-workspace/skills/` (orchestrator RO) = `/pool/skills/` (admin-console RW), System-Admin-managed, all teams. **Team**: `/workspace/teams/<teamId>/skills/` (RW), Team-Admin-managed, that team only. `orchestrator/skill-source.js` (`makeSkillSourceResolver`) copies each grant name from the team's pool when present, else global. `/api/skills` tags `source` = `pool`/`team`/`agent`; `POST /api/skills/promote-to-global` lifts a team skill into the global pool.

### Custom slash commands (#1796)

A granted skill may **carry Claude Code slash commands**: a `commands/*.md`
subdirectory in the skill dir. Wherever the skill is seeded or (de)activated, the
pure `orchestrator/skill-commands.js` projects those command files into the
agent workspace's `.claude/commands/<skill-slug>/<name>.md` (invoked as
`/<skill>:<name>`) as an **additive file sync, no respawn** — same path as skill
files (`seedAgentWorkspace` / `_syncSkillIntoWorkspace`, mirrored into the
isolated volume). The optional `description` front-matter is parsed and written
to `/workspace/agents/<id>/.agent-studio/seeded-commands.json`, exposed via
`GET /api/agents/:id/commands` for the #1797 autocomplete. There is **no** new
grant type and no ad-hoc authoring — commands ride the team-scoped skill grant.
On the **chat path only** (`agent-entrypoint.py`), a provisioned `/command`
expands natively in `claude --print` (verified — unlike the interactive-only
`AskUserQuestion`, #1712), and an **unknown** `/token` is neutralised (a leading
space is prepended) so it is answered as plain text instead of the CLI's
`Unknown command` short-circuit (AC2); a `slash.command` session-log event
records recognised/unknown. Full reference + headless findings:
`docs/slash-commands.md`.

### Knowledge digest (guaranteed context injection)

Part of the memory/knowledge unification (`docs/memory-knowledge-unification-concept.md`). Every KB-provisioned agent carries a generated **`knowledge-digest.md`** at its workspace root, `@`-imported by the seeded CLAUDE.md — so team conventions, converged (learned ≥2×) and recent lessons are **guaranteed in context at session start**; `knowledge search` is the long-tail complement. Deterministic (no LLM), ~3k-token budget, built by one pure module kept byte-identical on two sides: `orchestrator/knowledge-digest.js` ↔ `build_knowledge_digest()` in `agent-entrypoint.py` (golden fixture `knowledge-digest.fixture.json`; change both in one commit). Runtime rebuilds pre-invocation; `refreshKnowledgeClone` fans out post-KB-change. The `@`-import is gated (`knowledgeDigestRef`) on a provisioned checkout, so autonomy-0/KB-less agents see nothing.

**Semantic rerouting.** `agentKnowledgeDigestRef(id, env)` (autonomy ≥ 1 + provisioned checkout ≡ `_memory_model_unified()`) flips the digest import, swaps the Memory System section to the unified variant (episodic `context.md`/`log.md`/`users.md`; `facts.md`/`entities.md` read-only legacy — new semantic knowledge goes to `knowledge add`), and selects the unified memory hint. The legacy five-file model is the autonomy-0/KB-less/blueprint fallback. `users.md` is personal data — never shared, never migrated.

**Migration.** One-shot per unified agent (`migrateAgentMemoryToKb`, `orchestrator/memory-migration.js`): legacy `facts.md`/`entities.md` → KB entries in a dedicated shard `<kind>/shards/<agentId>.migrated.jsonl` (never the agent's own — avoids a two-writer race), committed+pushed; only after the push are files archived to `memory/_archive/`. **"in digest" indicator**: `buildKnowledgeDigest` returns `includedIds`; `GET /internal/teams/:teamId/knowledge/digest-status`; the Knowledge tab renders a green `digest` badge.

## MCP Servers (operator notes)

Reference servers ship under `mcp-servers/<id>/`; `make seed-mcp-servers` (chained from `make seed-pool`) syncs them to `/template-workspace/mcp-servers/`. Add a backend by: (1) drop a single-file MCP (stdio or http) under `mcp-servers/<id>/`, (2) `make seed-mcp-servers`, (3) register via the **MCP Servers** admin page + bind a credential + attach to environments. The `wiki/` reference server is tiny and dependency-free — clone it as a starting point.
